# ESP-IDF menuconfig options vs esp-ndf

Generated by `tools/idf_kconfig_inventory.py` from the local IDF tree (ESP-IDF v5.3.5): every option a user can set
for the ESP32-S3. It excludes hidden symbols, SOC_* capabilities and options for other chips.

| Status | Options | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| done | 106 | esp-ndf has an equivalent (named in the table) |
| planned | 638 | on the TODO list (docs/TODO.md) |
| wireless later | 1669 | radio / network stacks, after peripherals (rule 1) |
| n/a | 310 | not applicable by design or no such hardware on S3 |
| **total** | **2723** | |

| `SECURE_FLASH_PSEUDO_ROUND_FUNC_STRENGTH_HIGH` High | Bootloader config > Security features > Potentially insecure options | bool | n/a | no second-stage bootloader: the ROM loads the esp-ndf image directly |
| `SECURE_DISABLE_ROM_DL_MODE` UART ROM download mode (Permanently disabled (recommended)) | Bootloader config > Security features > Potentially insecure options | bool | n/a | no second-stage bootloader: the ROM loads the esp-ndf image directly |
| `SECURE_ENABLE_SECURE_ROM_DL_MODE` UART ROM download mode (Permanently switch to Secure mode (recommended)) | Bootloader config > Security features > Potentially insecure options | bool | n/a | no second-stage bootloader: the ROM loads the esp-ndf image directly |
| `SECURE_INSECURE_ALLOW_DL_MODE` UART ROM download mode (Enabled (not recommended)) | Bootloader config > Security features > Potentially insecure options | bool | n/a | no second-stage bootloader: the ROM loads the esp-ndf image directly |
